2]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC 324|UFC 324: Gaethje vs. Pimblett]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Royval vs. Kape''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 73''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 112''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on December 13, 2025, at the [[UFC Apex]] in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-09-01|title=Brandon Royval vs. Manel Kape to headline final UFC event on ESPN before Paramount move|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/09/01/brandon-royval-vs-manel-kape-headlines-last-ufc-card-espn-exit/85930230007/|access-date=2025-09-01|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was the final UFC broadcast under the U.S. media rights agreement with [[ESPN]] as beginning in 2026, UFC live event coverage will transition to [[Paramount Skydance]].\n\nA flyweight bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger (also former [[Legacy_Fighting_Alliance#LFA_Flyweight_Championship|LFA Flyweight Champion]]) [[Brandon Royval]] and former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Bantamweight Championship|RIZIN Bantamweight Champion]] [[Manel Kape]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2025-09-01|title=Flyweight contenders booked for third time in UFC's final event of 2025|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Flyweight-Contenders-Booked-for-Third-Time-in-UFCs-Final-Event-of-2025-198248|access-date=2025-09-01|publisher=sherdog.com}} The pairing was originally expected to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Kape vs. Almabayev]] in March 2025, but Royval had to withdraw due to multiple concussions.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/02/ufc-news-brandon-royval-vs-manel-kape-canceled|title=UFC Fight Night 253 main event canceled: Brandon Royval withdraws from Manel Kape fight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-02-05|access-date=2025-02-05}} They were later rebooked to compete at [[UFC 317]] in June 2025; however, Kape withdrew due to a broken foot.{{Cite web|author=Damon Martin|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/6/9/24446192/manel-kape-out-joshua-van-expected-to-face-brandon-royval-at-ufc-317|title=Manel Kape out, Joshua Van expected to face Brandon Royval at UFC 317|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2025-06-09|access-date=2025-06-09}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between former [[Legacy_Fighting_Alliance#LFA_Women's_Flyweight_Championship|LFA Women's Flyweight Champion]] Jamey-Lyn Horth and Tereza Bledá took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/article/tereza-bleda-vs-jamey-lyn-134843975.html|title=Tereza Bleda vs. Jamey-Lyn Horth set for UFC Vegas 112 clash|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Garrett Kerman|date=2025-09-18|access-date=2025-09-18}} They were originally scheduled to compete at [[UFC on ESPN: Usman vs. Buckley]] in June but Bledá was forced to withdraw from the fight due to a staph infection.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/4/2/24399969/jamey-lyn-horth-vs-tereza-bleda-set-for-ufc-atlanta|title=Jamey-Lyn Horth vs. Tereza Bleda set for UFC Atlanta|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2025-04-02|access-date=2025-04-02}}{{Cite web|title=Smůla pokračuje. Česká bojovnice musela v UFC zrušit další zápas|url=https://www.sport.cz/clanek/bojove-sporty-mma-smula-pokracuje-ceska-bojovnice-musela-v-ufc-zrusit-dalsi-zapas-5245309|date=2025-06-03|access-date=2025-06-04|publisher=sport.cz|author=David Suchý}} {{in lang|cs}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Luana Santos]] and Melissa Croden took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/10/29/melissa-croden-faces-luana-santos-at-ufc-vegas-112/|title=Melissa Croden Faces Luana Santos at UFC Vegas 112|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2025-10-29|access-date=2025-10-29}} The pairing was originally set for [[UFC on ESPN: Bonfim vs. Brown]] in November, but was moved to this card for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|title=Mudança no card! Luana Santos ganha nova adversária para o UFC Vegas lang|pt-br}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Gillian Robertson]] and [[Iasmin Lucindo]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|author=Jay Anderson|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/09/07/iasmin-lucindo-vs-gillian-robertson-booked-for-ufc-vegas-112/|title=Iasmin Lucindo vs. Gillian Robertson Booked for UFC Vegas 112|publisher=cagesidepress.com|date=2025-09-07|access-date=2025-09-07}} However, Lucindo withdrew due to an undisclosed injury and was replaced by former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] challenger [[Amanda Lemos]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.richmond-news.com/national-sports/croden-fight-shifted-to-december-adding-a-third-canadian-to-ufc-fight-night-show-11419893|title=Croden fight card by the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] on the day of the event due to a mouth injury and the bout was rescheduled for March 14, 2026, at [[UFC Fight Night 269]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Amanda-Lemos-vs-Gillian-Robertson-Postponed-to-March-14-199420|title=Amanda Lemos vs. Gillian Robertson postponed to March 14|author=Sayan Nag|date=2025-12-13|access-date=2026-01-06|publisher=sherdog.com}}\n\n[[Melsik Baghdasaryan]] was expected to face Joanderson Brito in a featherweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/two-banger-fights-added-final-ufc-event-2025|title=Two banger UFC fights reportedly added to final UFC event of 2025|publisher=si.com|author=Drew '''''UFC on ESPN+ 119''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 11, 2025, at the [[Farmasi Arena]], in [[Rio de Janeiro]], Brazil.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/7/4/24461584/ufc-plans-october-event-rio-de-janeiro-brazil-fight-night|title=UFC plans October event in Rio de Janeiro|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2025-07-04|access-date=2025-07-15}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event marked the promotion's 13th visit to Rio de Janeiro, its first since [[UFC 301]] in May 2024, and the first Fight Night hosted in the city since [[UFC Fight Night: Maia vs. LaFlare]] in May 2015.\n\nA lightweight bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Charles Oliveira]] and [[Rafael Fiziev]] was scheduled to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc-news/308078/charles-oliveira-vs-rafael-fiziev-announced-for-ufc-rio-main-event|title=Charles Oliveira vs. Rafael Fiziev announced for UFC Rio main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2025-08-07|access-date=2025-08-07}} However, Fiziev withdrew from the bout due to a knee injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/404929/rafael-fiziev-out-ufc-rio-main-event-fight-with-charles-oliveira|title=Rafael Fiziev out of UFC Rio main event fight with Charles Oliveira|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2025-09-19|access-date=2025-09-19}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/09/24/ufc-rio-rafael-fiziev-reveals-injury-withdrawal/86326745007|title=Rafael Fiziev opens up on severity of injury that forced UFC Rio withdrawal|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2025-09-24|access-date=2025-09-24}} He was replaced by former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Featherweight championship|KSW Featherweight]] and [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Lightweight championship|Lightweight Champion]] [[Mateusz Gamrot]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/09/23/charles-oliveira-meets-mateusz-gamrot-new-ufc-rio-de-janeiro-main-event/86316435007/|title=Charles Oliveira gets Mateusz Gamrot as new UFC Fight Night 261 main event opponent|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-09-23|access-date=2025-09-23}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Light Heavyweight Championship|LFA Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Ryan Spann]] and [[Valter Walker]] was reportedly scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|title=Walter Walker is on track to fight at UFC Rio; see opponent|url=https://www.superlutas.com.br/noticias/325654/valter-walker-ryan-spann-ufc-rio-veja-adversario/|date=2025-08-02|access-date=2025-08-26|publisher=superlutas.com.br|author=Velber Viana}} scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Rio-Gets-Brazil-vs-Argentina-Welterweight-Showdown-198008|title=UFC Rio gets Brazil vs. Argentina welterweight Álvarez]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.abc.es/deportes/mma/joel-alvarez-peleara-vicente-luque-ufc-rio-20250922173013-nt.html|title=Joel Álvarez peleará contra Vicente Luque en el UFC de Río de Janeiro arts event in 2025}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name= UFC 320: Ankalaev vs. Pereira 2\n|promotion= [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|image= UFC 320 poster.jpg\n|date= {{start date|2025|10|4}}\n|venue= web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/05/ufc-320-post-event-facts-alex-pereira-merab-dvalishvili-make-history-champions/86498411007/|title=UFC 320 post-event facts: Alex Pereira, Merab Dvalishvili make history in title wins|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-10-05|access-date=2025-10-05}}\n|gate= $8,840,134\n|previousevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Ulberg vs. Reyes]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Oliveira vs. Gamrot]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 320: Ankalaev vs. Pereira 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 4, 2025, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/ufc-320-announced-for-october-at-t-mobile-arena-in-las-vegas/|title=UFC 320 announced for October at T-Mobile Arena in Las Vegas|publisher=sportsnet.ca|author=Staff|date=2025-06-07|access-date=2025-06-07}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was originally expected to take place in [[Guadalajara]], Mexico on September 13 and serve as the third annual \"Noche UFC\" event commemorating [[Independence Day (Mexico)|Mexico's independence day]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2025/03/ufc-320-noche-guadalajara-mexico-announcement|title=Noche UFC heads to Mexico for first time as UFC 320 set for Guadalajara|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-03-29|access-date=2025-03-29}} However, due to the construction of Arena Guadalajara being stalled, the card was moved to [[San Antonio, Texas]] and shifted to a [[UFC Fight Night: Lopes vs. Silva|Fight Night]].{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/05/31/noche-ufc-moved-from-guadalajara-to-san-antonio-texas/|title=Noche UFC moved from Guadalajara to San Antonio, Texas|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Eddie Law|date=2025-05-31|access-date=2025-05-31}} The promotion announced a week later that the numbered event would be moved to October and took place in Las Vegas.\n\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] rematch between current champion [[Magomed Ankalaev]] and former champion [[Alex Pereira]] (also former [[List of UFC champions#Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]], former [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Middleweight Championship|Glory Middleweight]] and [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Light Heavyweight Championship|Light Heavyweight Champion]]) headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2025/7/22/24472558/ufc-320-ankalaev-vs-pereira-2-main-event-set-merab-vs-sandhagen-co-headlines-las-vegas-card|title=UFC 320: Ankalaev vs. Pereira 2 main event set, Merab vs. Sandhagen co-headlines Las Vegas card|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Alexander Behunin|date=2025-07-22|access-date=2025-07-22}} They previously competed at [[UFC 313]] where Ankalaev defeated Pereira for the championship via unanimous decision.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/03/09/ufc-313-magomed-ankalaev-rains-on-poatans-parade-dethrones-alex-pereira/|title=UFC 313: Magomed Ankalaev Rains on Poatan’s Parade, Dethrones Alex Pereira|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2025-03-09|access-date=2025-03-09}} Former champion (also the inaugural [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Light Heavyweight Championship|Rizin Light Heavyweight Champion]]) [[Jiří Procházka]], who met former title challenger [[Khalil Rountree Jr.]] at the event, served as backup and potential replacement for this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2025/7/28/24476157/jiri-prochazka-set-to-back-up-magomed-ankalaev-vs-alex-pereira-2-at-ufc-320|title=Jiri Prochazka set to back up Magomed Ankalaev vs. Alex Pereira 2 at UFC 320|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2025-07-28|access-date=2025-07-28}}\n\nIn addition, a [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Merab Dvalishvili]] and former interim title challenger [[Cory Sandhagen]] co-headlined the event.\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Daniel Santos (fighter)|Daniel Santos]] and Yoo Joo-sang was scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Ulberg vs. Reyes]] a week before, but it was eventually moved to this event due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/ufc-perth-adds-three-more-australian-fighters-to-fight-night-card/|title=UFC Perth adds three more Australian fighters to Fight Night card|publisher=sportsnet.ca|author=Staff|date=2025-08-26|access-date=2026-03-26}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/406620/daniel-santos-wants-to-flatline-zombie-jr-at-ufc-320-for-first-knockout-in-4-years|title=Daniel Santos wants to flatline Zombie Jr. at UFC 320 for first knockout in 4 years|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2025-10-05|access-date=2026-03-26}}\n\n[[Ateba Gautier]] and former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Middleweight Championship|LFA Middleweight Champion]] Osman Diaz were scheduled to meet in a middleweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|date=2025-07-23|title=UFC 318 star Ateba Gautier reportedly books UFC 320 return just days after brutal knockout win |url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2025/07/23/ufc-318-star-ateba-gautier-reportedly-books-ufc-320-return-just-days-after-brutal-knockout-win/|access-date=2025-07-23|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Curtis Calhoun}} However, Diaz pulled out from the event due to a medical issue and was replaced by promotional newcomer Tre'ston Vines.{{Cite web|author=Nolan King|title=Fighter withdraws from UFC 320 six days prior to pay-per-view event|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/09/29/ufc-320-ozzy-diaz-vs-ateba-gautier-withdrawal/86430098007/|access-date=2025-09-29|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}}{{Cite web|title=Ateba Gautier receives new opponent, remains on UFC 320 card|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Ateba-Gautier-Receives-New-Opponent-Remains-on-UFC-320-Card-198538|date=2025-09-30|access-date=2025-10-01|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[The Ultimate Fighter: Heavy Hitters]] featherweight winner [[Macy Chiasson]] weighed in at 137.5 pounds, one and a half pounds over the women's bantamweight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight and she was fined a percentage of her purse, which went to her opponent former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] and [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Yana Santos]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/03/ufc-320-ankalaev-pereira-dvalishvili-sandhagen-title-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream/86481602007/|title=UFC 320 weigh-in results: One heavy in Vegas, but title fighters on point|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2025-10-03|access-date=2025-10-03}} The featherweight bout between [[Daniel Santos (fighter)|Daniel Santos]] and Yoo Joo-sang was also changed to a catchweight bout of 153 pounds.\n\n==Event==\n===Main card===\n\n==== Procházka vs. Rountree Jr. ====\nPrior to the fight, commentators generally agreed that the fight was a tossup, with both fighters noted as having significant knockout power.{{Cite web |date=2025-10-02 |title=Matchup Spotlight {{!}} Jiří Procházka vs Khalil Rountree Jr. {{!}} UFC |url=https://www.ufc.com/news/matchup-spotlight-jiri-prochazka-vs-khalil-rountree-jr-ufc-320 |access-date=2026-09-12 |website=www.ufc.com |language=en}} The fight saw Rountree dominate the first two rounds, outstriking Procházka, who attempted to engage with little success. Procházka turned the tide of the fight by the third round by managing to land several combinations on Rountree, finishing him off with a spinning elbow and repeated punches until Rountree was knocked out cold.{{Cite web |last=Staff |date=2025-10-05 |title=UFC 320: Jiri Prochazka Storms Back, Stops Khalil Rountree Jr in Third |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/10/04/ufc-320-jiri-prochazka-storms-back-stops-khalil-rountree-jr-in-third/ |access-date=2026-09-12 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\n====Dvalishvili vs. Sandhagen ====\nThe fight was predicted by most outlets to be another win by Dvalishvili, noting his wrestling expertise and impressive cardio. However, Sandhagen's striking ability and aggression was noted as potentially being able to change the tide of the fight.{{Cite web|author=Andrew Richardson|date=2025-10-03|title=Merab Dvalishvili vs. Cory Sandhagen: Odds, full fight preview and prediction|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/article/merab-dvalishvili-vs-cory-sandhagen-130500790.html|access-date=2025-10-25|publisher=sports.yahoo.com}}\n\nThe bout started out with a close round where Sandhagen saw some success against Dvalishvili. Dvalishvili controlled the bout against Sandhagen starting in the second round, dropping Sandhagen and landing over 8 takedowns over 13 attempts. Dvalishvili later expressed surprise he could not finish Sandhagen in the second round, where he landed over 33 strikes compared to just 2 by Sandhagen.{{Cite web|date=2025-10-05|title=Dvalishvili's reign continues in rout of Sandhagen|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/46493053/dvalishvili-rolls-decisive-win-sandhagen-ufc-320|access-date=2025-10-25|publisher=espn.com|author=Jeff Wagenheim}}\n\n====Ankalaev vs. Pereira====\nBefore the fight, many commentators and sites predicted a similar result to the previous fight, where Ankalaev out-struck Pereira standing up.{{Cite web|title=Magomed Ankalaev vs. Alex Pereira prediction: Who takes rematch at UFC 320?|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/03/ufc-320-magomed-ankalaev-vs-alex-pereira-pick-odds-time/86497554007/|date=2025-10-03|access-date=2025-10-18|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Dan Tom}} Pereira claimed that he only fought at \"40 percent\" of his strength in the previous fight, dealing with an injured hand.{{Cite web|date=2025-09-27|title=Pereira says he fought Ankalaev at '40 percent'|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/46391411/pereira-says-was-40-percent-ufc-title-loss-ankalaev|access-date=2025-10-20|publisher=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto}}\n\nContrary to predictions of a close match, the fight saw Pereira immediately dominating Ankalaev, backing him to the fence and attacking him with calf kicks and a right hand.{{Cite web|date=2025-10-05|title=UFC 320 results and analysis for Ankalaev vs. Pereira 2|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/46453040/ufc-320-live-results-analysis-magomed-ankalaev-alex-pereira-merab-dvalishvili-cory-sandhagen|access-date=2025-10-18|publisher=espn.com|author=Staff}} After being hit with a right hand, Ankalaev unsuccessfully shot for a takedown, leading Pereira to bombard him with elbows and punches until the fight was ruled a technical knockout eighty seconds in.{{Cite web|date=2025-10-05|title=UFC 320 -- Magomed Ankalaev vs. Alex Pereira 2: Results, winners, highlights, fight card, complete guide|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-320-magomed-ankalaev-vs-alex-pereira-2-results-winners-highlights-fight-card-complete-guide/|access-date=2025-10-18|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Shakiel Mahjouri}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Alex Pereira]]\n|def.\n|[[Magomed Ankalaev]] (c)\n|TKO (punches and Championship]] that took place on October 25, 2025, at the [[Etihad Arena]] in [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/07/01/ufc-321-abu-dhabi-etihad-arena-october-25-in-abu-dhabi/84427386007/|title=UFC 321 announced for Oct. 25 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2025-07-01|access-date=2025-07-01}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's 22nd visit to Abu Dhabi and first since [[UFC on ABC: Whittaker vs. de Ridder]] in July 2025.\n\nA [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Tom Aspinall]] and former interim champion [[Ciryl Gane]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmanews.com/news/ufc/tom-aspinall-to-defend-ufc-heavyweight-title-vs-ciryl-gane-at-ufc-321/|title=Tom Aspinall to defend UFC Heavyweight title vs. Ciryl Gane at UFC 321|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Thomas Albano|date=2025-07-22|access-date=2025-07-22}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] [[Virna Jandiroba]] and [[Mackenzie Dern]] took place in the co-main event. Current two-time champion [[Zhang Weili]] vacated her title in order to challenge for the [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]].{{Cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/08/28/ufc-322-valetina-shevchenko-vs-zhang-weili-new-york/85869503007/|title=UFC champ vacates title for superfight showdown; contenders to fight for vacant title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-08-28|access-date=2025-08-28}} The pairing previously met at [[UFC 256]] in December 2020 which Dern won by unanimous decision.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/12/12/22172119/ufc-256-results-mackenzie-dern-showcases-improved-striking-to-win-unanimous-decision-virna-jandiroba|title=UFC 256 results: Mackenzie Dern showcases improved striking to win unanimous decision over Virna Jandiroba|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-12-12|access-date=2025-08-28}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Umar Nurmagomedov]] and [[Mario Bautista]] took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/08/28/ufc-321-umar-nurmagomedov-vs-mario-bautista/85870564007/|title=UFC 321: Umar Nurmagomedov gets rising contender in Abu Dhabi|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-08-28|access-date=2025-08-30}} They were originally reported to be competing in the main event of [[UFC Fight Night: de Ridder vs. Allen]] one week prior but were moved to this event for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://clutchpoints.com/ufc/umar-nurmagomedovs-return-fight-against-mario-bautista-is-set-for-ufc-vancouver-main-event|title=Umar Nurmagomedov’s return fight against Mario Bautista is set for UFC Vancouver main event|publisher=clutchpoints.com|author=Garrett Kerman|date=2025-08-04|access-date=2025-08-04}}\n\nAbdul-Kareem Al-Selwady and Matheus Camilo were expected to meet in a lightweight bout on the preliminary card, but Al-Selwady pulled out less than 48 hours before the event and the pairing was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/400949/matheus-camilo-abdul-kareem-al-selwady-fight-ufc-321|title=Matheus Camilo, Abdul-Kareem Al-Selwady agree to fight at UFC 321|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2025-08-26|access-date=2025-10-23}}{{Cite Martin|date=2025-10-23|access-date=2025-10-23}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, two fighters missed weight:{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/23/ufc-321-abu-dhabi-tom-aspinall-ciryl-gane-jandiroba-dern-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream/86856151007|title=UFC 321 weigh-in results: Title fights official but two heavy on scale|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2025-10-24|access-date=2025-10-24}}\n*José Miguel Delgado weighed in at 147 pounds, one pound over the featherweight non-title fight limit.\n*Azat Maksum weighed in at 129 pounds, three pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit.\n\nDelgado and Maksum's bouts proceeded at catchweight. Both fighters were fined a percentage of their individual purses which went to their opponents [[Nathaniel Wood (fighter)|Nathaniel Wood]] (20 percent) and Mitch Raposo (30 percent), respectively.\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Tom Aspinall]] (c)\n|vs.\n|[[Ciryl Gane]]\n|NC (accidental eye poke)\n|1\n|4:35\n|For the [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]]. However, Gaziev withdrew from that event due to a broken finger sustained during training.{{Cite web|url=https://athlonsports.com/mma/ufc-serghei-spivac-vs-shamil-gaziev-fight-announced-ufc-316|title=UFC 316 fight card levels up – heavyweights join the main card|publisher=athlonsports.com|author=Prince Horton|date=2025-04-01|access-date=2025-04-01}}{{Cite web|url=https://vringe.com/mma/news/188256-ne-gaziev-u-spivaka-novyy-sopernik-po-ufc-316.htm|title=Не Газиев. 110''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 121''''') was a mixed martial arts event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 1, 2025, at the [[UFC Apex]] in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/09/20/ufc-las-vegas-apex-tmobile-schedule-2025/86264647007|title=UFC lays and [[David Onama]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/404862/featherweight-main-event-targeted-for-ufc-vegas-110|title=Featherweight main event targeted for UFC Vegas 110|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2025-09-20|access-date=2025-09-20}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Heavyweight Championship|LFA Heavyweight Champion]] [[Waldo Cortes-Acosta]] and [[2022 in Professional Fighters League#2022 PFL Heavyweight playoffs|2022 PFL Heavyweight Tournament]] winner [[Ante Delija]] was initially expected to serve as the event headliner, but was later withdrawn from that position for unspecified reasons.{{Cite web|author=Andrew Richardson|date=2025-09-13|title=Hard-hitting Heavyweight newcomer scores UFC main event in just second appearance|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-news/389137/hard-hitting-heavyweight-newcomer-scores-ufc-main-event-in-just-second-appearance|access-date=2025-09-13|publisher=mmamania.com}}\n\nA middleweight bout between Nick Klein and undefeated promotional newcomer Donte Johnson was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-main-event-onama-garcia-espn-fight-night|title=UFC reportedly books unexpected headliner for one of ESPN's last UFC Fight Night cards|publisher=si.com|author=Drew Beaupre|date=2025-09-19|access-date=2025-09-19}} However, Klein withdrew from the fight for unknown reasons and was replaced by Sedriques Dumas.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-321-adds-surprise-fight-of-the-night-contender-haqparast-salkilld|title=UFC 321 reportedly adds surprise Fight of the Night contender on very short notice|publisher=si.com|author=Drew Beaupre|date=2025-10-18|access-date=2025-10-18}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Bantamweight Championship|LFA Bantamweight Champions]] [[Miles Johns]] and Muin Gafurov was scheduled for this event. However, Gafurov withdrew for undisclosed reasons, and Johns was subsequently rescheduled to face a new opponent one week later at [[UFC Fight Night: Bonfim vs. Brown]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-fight-announcements/399438/switcheroo-ufc-vegas-110-coughs-up-bantamweight-bout-spits-it-to-ufc-vegas-111|title=Switcheroo! following fighters received $50,000 bonuses.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-bonuses-and-awards/400185/ufc-vegas-110-official-post-fight-bonus-winners-results-espn-garcia-johnson-cortes-acosta|title=Bonuses! Waldo Cortes-Acosta’s wild comeback knockout finish leads $50K winners - UFC Vegas 110|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Dan Hiergesell|date=2025-11-02|access-date=2025-11-02}}\n*'''Fight of the Night:''' No bonus awarded.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Steve Garcia''', '''Waldo Cortes-Acosta''', '''Allan Nascimento''', and '''Donte Johnson'''\n\n==Isaac Dulgarian's betting scandal==\nOn November 3, two days after the conclusion of the event, the UFC released Isaac Dulgarian because of his suspicious activity in his bout against Yadier del Valle. Unusual betting activity on the contest was reported a few hours before the event, as Dulgarian dropped from a -250 favorite to -154 just prior to fight time, according to BestFightOdds.com. He also performed poorly and showed questionable defense during his first-round loss. The promotion released a statement:{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/11/03/ufc-cuts-isaac-dulgarian-fight-fixing-investigation-illegal-gambling-mma-allegations/87071802007|title=UFC cuts Isaac Dulgarian, announces investigation of fight-fixing allegations|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2025-11-03|access-date=2025-11-03}}\n\n{{quote|Like many professional sports organizations, UFC works with an independent betting integrity service to monitor wagering activity on our events. Our betting integrity partner, IC360, monitors wagering on every UFC event and is conducting a thorough review of the facts surrounding the Dulgarian vs. del Valle bout on Saturday, November 1. We take these allegations very seriously, and along with the health and safety of our fighters, nothing is more important than the integrity of our sport.}}\n\nUFC CEO [[Dana White]] later announced that the organization called the FBI and was cooperating with them as part of the investigation.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/453508/dana-white-reveals-ufc-met-with-fbi-over-fight-fixing-allegations-it-definitely-doesnt-look-good-for-isaac-dulgarian|title=Dana White reveals UFC met with FBI over fight-fixing allegations, ‘it definitely doesn’t look good’ for Isaac Dulgarian|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2025-11-04|access-date=2025-11-04}}{{cite web | title=UFC meets with FBI over suspicious bets on Isaac Dulgarian fight | url=https://www.bbc.co.uk/sport/mixed-martial-arts/articles/cj6nwp4gggxo |publisher=BBC Sport |accessdate=2025-11-30}} On November 13, Dulgarian was temporarily suspended by the [[Nevada Athletic Commission]], who will vote on extending that suspension six days later on their monthly meeting. There’s a second measure on the docket with the commission also voting on withholding his purse from the fight as well.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/455975/ex-ufc-fighter-isaac-dulgarian-suspended-by-nevada-athletic-commission-amid-fight-fixing-allegations|title=Ex-UFC fighter Isaac Dulgarian suspended by Nevada Athletic Commission amid fight-fixing allegations|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2025-11-13|access-date=2025-11-14}}\n\n== See also ==\n*[[2025 in UFC]]\n*[[List of current States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportsnet.ca/ufc/article/two-champion-vs-champion-fights-to-headline-ufc-322-in-new-york/|title=Two champion vs. champion fights to headline UFC 322 in New November 2024.\n\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Jack Della Maddalena]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Islam Makhachev]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/08/28/ufc-322-jack-della-maddalena-vs-islam-makhachev-new-york/85867955007/|title=UFC 322 main event announced: Jack Della Maddalena vs. Islam Makhachev|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-08-28|access-date=2025-08-28}} Makhachev vacated his lightweight title in May in order to move up to welterweight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2025/5/13/24429594/dana-white-drops-ufc-317-fight-ilia-topuria-vs-charles-oliveira-headlines-vacant-lightweight-title|title=Vegas heat! Dana White drops UFC 317 fight card - Ilia Topuria vs. Charles Oliveira headlines for vacant lightweight title|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Alexander Behunin|date=2025-05-13|access-date=2025-05-13}}\n\nIn addition, a [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] bout between current two-time champion [[Valentina Shevchenko]] and former two-time [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Zhang Weili]] co-headlined the event. Zhang vacated her strawweight title in October in order to challenge for the flyweight title.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/08/28/ufc-322-valetina-shevchenko-vs-zhang-weili-new-york/85869503007/|title=UFC champ vacates title for super-fight showdown; contenders to fight for vacant title|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-08-28|access-date=2025-08-28}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between Malcolm Wellmaker and Serhiy Sidey was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2025/10/09/10-0-ufc-rising-star-gets-his-wish-to-fight-on-jack-della-maddalena-vs-islam-makhachev-card-at-msg/|title=10-0 UFC rising star gets his wish to fight on Jack Della Maddalena vs Islam Makhachev card at MSG|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Harvey Leonard|date=2025-10-09|access-date=2025-10-09}} However, Sidey withdrew due to a shoulder injury and was replaced by Cody Haddon.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/13/ufc-322-serhiy-sidey-injured-malcolm-wellmaker/86680868007/|title=UFC 322: Serhiy Sidey out of matchup vs. Malcolm Wellmaker|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells and Nolan King|date=2025-10-13|access-date=2025-10-13}}{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/16/prospect-malcolm-wellmaker-gets-new-opponent-for-ufc-322/86728172007|title=Prospect Malcolm Wellmaker gets new opponent for UFC 322|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura, Mike Bohn and Nolan King|date=2025-10-16|access-date=2025-10-16}} Two days before the event, Haddon also withdrew due to an injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer Ethyn Ewing. The bout was subsequently shifted to the featherweight division.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/456002/ufc-322-malcolm-wellmaker-gets-new-opponent-on-just-2-days-notice|title=UFC 322: Malcolm Wellmaker gets new opponent on just 2 days' notice|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2025-11-14|access-date=2025-11-14}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Beneil Dariush]] weighed in at 157.2 pounds, 1.2 pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight and he was fined 25 percent of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Benoît Saint Denis]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/456088/ufc-322-weigh-in-results-championship-fights-set-1-veteran-misses|title=UFC 322 weigh-in results: Championship fights set, 1 veteran misses|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. '''''UFC on ESPN+ 122''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 8, 2025, at the [[UFC Apex]] in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/09/20/ufc-las-vegas-apex-tmobile-schedule-2025/86264647007|title=UFC lays groundwork Alliance#LFA Welterweight Championship|LFA Welterweight Champion]] [[Gabriel Bonfim]] and [[Randy Brown (fighter)|Randy Brown]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|author=Staff|date=2025-09-27|title=Gabriel Bonfim vs. Randy Brown shifts to November as UFC Vegas 111 headliner|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/09/27/gabriel-bonfim-vs-randy-brown-shifts-to-november-as-ufc-vegas-111-headliner/|access-date=2025-09-27|publisher=cagesidepress.com}} They were originally scheduled to compete at [[UFC Fight Night: Oliveira vs. Gamrot]] one month before, but the bout was moved to this event in order to serve as the main event.{{Cite web|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2025-08-20|title=Gabriel Bonfim booked for UFC Rio return after win over Stephen Thompson|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/400919/gabriel-bonfim-ufc-rio-return-after-win-stephen-thompson|access-date=2025-09-02|publisher=mmafighting.com}}\n\nAustin Bashi was expected to face [[Jamall Emmers]] in a featherweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://clutchpoints.com/ufc/marco-tulio-vs-christian-leroy-duncan-set-for-ufc-vegas-111-on-november-8th|title=Marco Tulio vs. Christian Leroy Duncan set for UFC Vegas 111 on November 8th|publisher=clutchpoints.com|author=Garrett Kerman|date=2025-09-23|access-date=2025-09-23}} However, Bashi pulled out due to an injury and was replaced by [[Hyder Amil]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/13/ufc-vegas-111-austin-bashi-injured-jamall-emmers-vs-hyder-amil/86682987007|title=UFC Fight Night 264: Jamall Emmers lands new opponent after Austin Bashi injury|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells, Nolan King and Farah Hannounn|date=2025-09-23|access-date=2025-10-13}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Luana Santos]] and Dariya Zheleznyakova was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|title=UFC encaminha Luana Santos vs Darya Zheleznyakova para o dia 8 de novembro|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/ufc-encaminha-luana-santos-vs-darya-zheleznyakova-para-o-dia-8-de-novembro|date=2025-08-28|access-date=2025-08-28|publisher=agfight.com|author=Neri Fung}} {{in lang|pt-br}} However, Zheleznyakova withdrew from the fight for unknown reasons and was replaced by Melissa Croden.{{Cite web|title=Mudança no card! Luana Santos ganha nova adversária para o UFC Vegas 111|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/mudanca-no-card-luana-santos-ganha-nova-adversaria-para-o-ufc-vegas-111/|date=2025-10-23|access-date=2025-10-26|publisher=agfight.com|author=Geovanne Peçanha}} {{in lang|pt-br}} The rescheduled bout between Santos and Croden was later moved to [[UFC on ESPN: Royval vs. Kape]] in December.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/10/29/melissa-croden-faces-luana-santos-at-ufc-vegas-112/|title=Melissa Croden Faces Luana Santos at UFC Vegas 112|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2025-10-29|access-date=2025-10-29}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Bantamweight Championship|LFA Bantamweight Champion]] [[Miles Johns]] and [[Daniel Marcos]] took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/28/ufc-fight-night-264-miles-johns-vs-daniel-marcos-bantamweight-mma-fight-booking-felipe-lima-injury/86950923007/|title=UFC Fight Night 264 injury leads to lineup shuffle at bantamweight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matt Erickson and Nolan King|date=2025-10-28|access-date=2025-10-28}} Johns was initially booked to face fellow former LFA champion Muin Gafurov one week prior at [[UFC Fight Night: Garcia vs. Onama]], but Gafurov withdrew for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-fight-announcements/399438/switcheroo-ufc-vegas-110-coughs-up-bantamweight-bout-spits-it-to-ufc-vegas-111|title=Switcheroo! However, the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] declined the substitution because Johnson was under a two-week medical suspension.{{Cite web|date=2025-11-04|title=Undefeated Contender Series ‘Lock Jaw’ will fight twice in two weeks ... on just four days notice|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-news/400720/undefeated-contender-series-lock-jaw-will-fight-twice-in-two-weeks-on-just-four-days-notice-ufc-vegas-111|access-date=2025-11-05|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Alexander Behunin}}{{Cite web|date=2025-11-05|title=UFC fighter reportedly denied chance to break Khamzat Chimaev’s record with two wins in a week|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2025/11/05/ufc-fighter-reportedly-denied-chance-to-break-khamzat-chimaevs-record-with-two-wins-in-a-week/|access-date=2025-11-05|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Kyle Dimond}} As a result, [[Zachary Reese]] stepped in to face McVey in a catchweight bout at 195 pounds.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/453628/jackson-mcvey-now-meets-zach-reese-in-last-minute-shakeup-at-ufc-vegas-111|title=Jackson McVey now meets Zach Reese in last-minute shakeup at UFC Vegas 111|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2025-11-06|access-date=2025-11-06}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Ismael Bonfim weighed in at 161 pounds, five pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit. [[Paramount Skydance]] took effect in 2026, as the numbered events will instead be available to stream at no additional cost on [[Paramount+]] in the U.S. starting next year. The PPV model has been a hallmark of the promotion since its inception in 1993 and has been especially emphasized during the organization's seven-year broadcast partnership with [[ESPN]].{{Cite web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/mma/breaking-news/article/ufc-pay-per-view-model-will-still-exist-in-paramount-era-outside-of-us-market-says-exec-000949095.html|title=UFC pay-per-view model will still exist in Paramount era outside of U.S. market, says exec|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Uncrowned Staff|date=2025-09-06|access-date=2025-09-11}}\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Merab Dvalishvili]] and former champion [[Petr Yan]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/13/ufc-323-merab-dvalishvili-vs-petr-yan-rematch-main-event/86677550007|title=UFC 323 gets surprise headliner as Merab Dvalishvili makes quick turnaround|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-10-13|access-date=2025-10-13}} With a win, Dvalishvili would become the first UFC champion in history to defend a title four times in the same calendar year. The pairing previously met in March 2023 at [[UFC Fight Night: Yan vs. Dvalishvili]] which Dvalishvili won by unanimous decision.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/3/11/23635894/merab-dvalishvili-dominates-petr-yan-in-shutout-performance-in-ufc-las-vegas-main-event|title=Merab Dvalishvili dominates Petr Yan in shutout web|url=https://sports.yahoo.com/article/mansur-abdul-malik-set-clash-134123587.html|title=Mansur Abdul-Malik set for clash with Antonio Trocoli at UFC 323|publisher=sports.yahoo.com|author=Garrett was scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Tsarukyan vs. Hooker]].{{Cite web|date=2025-08-31|access-date=2025-08-31|title=Jan Błachowicz vs. Bogdan Guskov set for UFC Qatar debut|url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/jan-blachowicz-vs-bogdan-guskov-set-for-ufc-qatar-debut/|publisher=lowkickmma.com|author=Timothy Wheaton}} However, the pairing was moved to this event for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|author=Farah Hannoun and Nolan King|title=Jan Blachowicz meets rising contender at UFC 323|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/10/13/ufc-323-jan-blachowicz-vs-bogdan-guskov-booked/85927399007/|access-date=2025-10-13|date=2025-10-13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Terrance McKinney]] and [[Chris Duncan (fighter)|Chris Duncan]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2025/10/12/ufc-lightweight-with-16-first-round-finishes-in-17-wins-will-fight-at-the-final-espn-pay-per-view/|title=UFC lightweight with 16 first-round finishes in 17 Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/12/05/ufc-2026-schedule-first-quarter/87630149007/|title=UFC announces 2026 schedule for first Veretennikov.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Nikolay-Veretennikov-Replaces-Eric-Nolan-at-UFC-Fight-Night-266-199784|title=Nikolay Veretennikov replaces Eric Nolan at UFC Fight Night The fight was later rescheduled for [[UFC 334]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/466164/report-kayla-harrison-out-of-ufc-324-fight-against-amanda-nunes-postponed-to-later-date|title=Kayla Harrison out of UFC 324, fight against Amanda Nunes postponed to later date|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2026-01-14|access-date=2026-01-14}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship|UFC Women's Flyweight Champion]] [[Alexa Grasso]] and former two-time [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship|UFC Women's Strawweight Champion]] [[Rose Namajunas]] were expected to meet in a flyweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/11/27/ufc-324-fight-card-sean-omalley-song-yadong-rose-namajunas-alexa-grasso/87488327007/|title=UFC 324 fight card: Sean O'Malley returns, ex-women's champs collide|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2025-11-27|access-date=2025-11-27}} However, Grasso pulled out on December 3 due to injury and was replaced by [[Natália Silva (fighter)|Natália Silva]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Streaking-Flyweight-Contender-Steps-in-to-Face-Rose-Namajunas-at-UFC-324-199285|title=Streaking flyweight contender steps in to face Rose Namajunas at UFC 324|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Marcelo Alonso|date=2025-12-03|access-date=2025-12-03}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, two fighters missed weight:{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/01/23/ufc-324-justin-gaethje-vs-paddy-pimblett-interim-title-weigh-in-results-live-video-stream/88314933007|title=UFC 324 weigh-in results: Title fight set; Both fighters were fined 25 percent of their individual purses which went to their opponents former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Umar Nurmagomedov]] and [[Charles Johnson (fighter)|Charles Johnson]], respectively.\n\nAlso at the weigh-ins, Cameron Smotherman made weight for his bantamweight contest against [[The Return of The Ultimate Fighter: Team Volkanovski vs. Team Ortega]] bantamweight winner Ricky Turcios, but collapsed moments after stepping off the scale. The bout was subsequently canceled. The cancellation followed notable betting‑line movement on the matchup, and it was reported that some observers speculated Hernandez may have been dealing with an injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bodyslam.net/2026/01/24/michael-johnson-vs-alexander-hernandez-called-off-from-ufc-324-card-promotion-announces/|title=Michael Johnson vs. Alexander Hernandez Called Off from UFC 324 Card, Promotion Announces|publisher=bodyslam.net|author=Rami Afif|date=2026-01-25|access-date=2026-01-25}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-324-news-results-paramount-streaming-updates-mma/416774/something-or-nothing-unusual-betting-activity-raises-questions-over-ufc-324-undercard-fight-gaethje-vs-pimblett-johnson-hernandez|title=Something or nothing? promotion's eighth visit to Sydney and first since [[UFC 312]] in February 2025.\n\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] rematch between reigning two-time champion [[Alexander Volkanovski]] and former title challenger [[Diego Lopes (fighter)|Diego Lopes]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/11/27/ufc-325-alexander-volkanovski-vs-diego-lopes-2-booked-title-fight/87497276007/|title=Alexander Volkanovski vs. Diego Lopes 2 headlines UFC 325 in Sydney|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-11-27|access-date=2025-11-27}} The pair previously met at [[UFC 314]] in April 2025, where Volkanovski captured the vacant championship with a unanimous decision victory.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/04/13/alexander-volkanovski-becomes-two-time-champion-decisions-lopes/|title=Alexander Volkanovski becomes two-time champion, decisions Lopes|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Eddie Law|date=2025-04-13|access-date=2025-04-13}}\n\nA welterweight bout between Oban Elliott and Jonathan Micallef took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.foxsports.com.au/news/stacked-card-announced-for-ufcs-blockbuster-return-down-under/news-story/b36740de33e9a2d8a8f4154f8926340e|title=Kiwi in fight of the year contender, Aussies galore as stacked card for UFC's Sydney return revealed|publisher=foxsports.com.au|author=James Cooney|date=2025-12-07|access-date=2025-12-07}} The pairing was originally expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Ulberg vs. Reyes]] in September 2025, but was cancelled less than 48 hours before the event after Elliott withdrew due to illness.{{Cite web|url=https://www.australianmma.com.au/post/jono-micallef-eyes-statement-win-at-ufc-fight-night-perth-against-oban-elliot|title=Jono Micallef eyes statement win at UFC Fight Night Perth against Oban February.{{Cite web|url=https://www.index.hr/amp/sport/clanak/pomaknut-datum-sljedece-delijine-borbe-u-ufcu/2740744.aspx|title=Pomaknut datum sljedeće Delijine borbe u web|url=https://www.foxsports.com.au/news/stacked-card-announced-for-ufcs-blockbuster-return-down-under/news-story/b36740de33e9a2d8a8f4154f8926340e|title=Kiwi in fight of the year contender, Aussies 325|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2026-01-09|access-date=2026-01-09}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Jimmy Crute]] and [[Dustin Jacoby]] was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/3421071|title=Hooker-Saint Denis, Fiziev-Ruffy added to UFC 325|publisher=thescore.com|author=Nick Baldwin|date=2025-12-07|access-date=2025-12-07}} However, Crute withdrew due to a ruptured [[anterior cruciate ligament|ACL]] and the bout was cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/01/14/ufc-325-news-jimmy-crute-injured-out-vs-dustin-jacoby/88188917007/|title=UFC 325: Jimmy Crute announces ACL injury, out vs. Dustin Jacoby|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2026-01-14|access-date=2026-01-14}} Jacoby was then re-scheduled to compete the following week at [[UFC Fight Night: Bautista vs. Oliveira]].{{Cite web|url=http://www.pwinsiderxtra.com/ViewArticle.php?id=39766&p=1|title=Report: Uran Satybaldiev off UFC Vegas 113, Dustin Jacoby steps in|publisher=pwinsiderxtra.com|author=David Tees|date=2026-01-24|access-date=2026-01-29}}\n\nAdditionally, three [[Road to UFC Season 4]] finals took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightmag.com/ufc-325-full-fight-card-announced-hooker-tuivasa-crute-and-more|title=UFC 325 full fight card announced: Hooker, Tuivasa, Crute and more|publisher=fightmag.com|author=Parviz Iskenderov|date=2025-12-08|access-date=2025-12-09}} The flyweight tournament final between Aaron Tau and Namsrai Batbayar was also scheduled for the event. web|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/ufc-houston/ufc-houston-bonus-report-melquizael-costa-one-of-four-potn-winners/|title=UFC Houston bonus report: Melquizael Costa one of four 'POTN' Medić''', '''Melquizael Costa''', and '''Jacobe Smith'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn March 31, it was announced that Alibi Idiris has accepted a 12-month suspension after failing an in-competition test submitted to Combat Sports Anti-Doping (CSAD) on fight day. Idiris tested positive for [[hydrochlorothiazide]], a prohibited diuretic in the Diuretics and Masking Agents class of substances on the UFC Prohibited List. Furthermore, Idiris' victory against [[Ode' Osbourne]] was overturned. That decision came separately from the [[Texas Department of Licensing and Regulation]] (TDLR), which also lists hydrochlorothiazide as a banned substance.\n\n== See also ==\n{{Portal|Martial Moreno vs. Kavanagh]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Emmett vs. Vallejos]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 326: Holloway vs. Oliveira 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on March 7, 2026, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/12/05/ufc-326-max-holloway-vs-charles-oliveira-bmf-title-rematch-set-las-vegas/87608706007/|title=Max Holloway vs. Charles Oliveira BMF title rematch set for UFC 326|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2025-12-06|access-date=2025-12-06}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first featured official broadcast on [[CBS]] after the new media rights agreement with [[Paramount Skydance]] began in January, as the network partially aired the card during the final hour of the prelims along with the first hour of the main card. The remainder of the main card including the main event aired exclusively on [[Paramount+]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/471268/ufc-326-to-partially-air-on-cbs-in-first-crossover-to-network-tv-under-new-paramount-deal|title=UFC 326 to partially air on CBS in first crossover to network TV under new Paramount deal|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2026-02-10|access-date=2026-02-10}} The CBS simulcast marked the promotion's best linear television ratings in a decade. According to the [[Nielsen Media Research|Nielsen Ratings System]], the two-hour broadcast window averaged 2.47 million viewers, while the first hour of the main card averaged over 2.8 million. The broadcast peaked at 3.1 million viewers during the middleweight bout between [[Gregory Rodrigues]] and Brunno Ferreira.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/476284/ufc-326-gets-sizable-boost-from-simulcast-on-cbs-that-peaked-with-over-3-million-viewers|title=UFC 326 gets sizable boost from simulcast on CBS that peaked with over 3 million viewers|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2026-03-12|access-date=2026-03-12}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Max Holloway]] defended his symbolic \"[[List of UFC champions#BMF Title|BMF]]\" (''baddest [[motherfucker]]'') title against former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Charles Oliveira]] in a lightweight bout serving as the event's headliner. The two fighters previously met in August 2015 at [[UFC Fight Night: Holloway vs. Oliveira]], where Oliveira was defeated via first-round technical knockout in a featherweight bout after sustaining an apparent neck and shoulder injury during a takedown attempt, which rendered him unable to continue.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/06/max-holloway-vs-charles-oliveira-headlines-ufcs-debut-in-saskatoon-on-aug-23|title=Max Holloway vs. Charles Oliveira headlines UFC's debut in Saskatoon on Aug. Oliveira injury|publisher=mmajunkie.com.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2015-08-23|access-date=2025-12-06}} The injury was later reported as a minor neck injury.{{cite event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2026-03-06|access-date=2026-03-06}}\n\nDuring the event's broadcast, it was announced that the [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout that took place in March 2020 at [[UFC 248]] between then-champion [[Zhang Weili]] (who would go on to become a two-time champion) and former champion [[Joanna Jędrzejczyk]] would be inducted into the \"fight wing\" of the [[UFC Hall of Fame]]. It is the first women's bout to receive the honor and will be recognized during International Fight Week festivities in Las Vegas this summer. Zhang defended the title via unanimous decision in a fight that is widely regarded as the greatest to ever take place between two women in mixed martial arts history.{{Cite headlined the event.{{Cite web|author=Farah Hannoun|title=Israel Adesanya returns to fight Joe Pyfer in UFC Seattle headliner|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/01/13/ufc-seattle-israel-adesanya-vs-joe-pyfer-main-event-booked/88157480007/|date=2026-01-13|access-date=2026-01-13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between Nicolle Caliari and promotional newcomer Caroline Foro was originally scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-paramount-plus-news-hub-fight-cards-schedule/413469/fight-news-valter-walker-vs-marcin-tybura-booked-for-ufc-seattle-on-march-28|title=Fight news: Valter Walker vs. Marcin Tybura booked for UFC Seattle on March 28|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Alexander Behunin|date=2026-01-09|access-date=2026-01-09}} However, Caliari withdrew for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by Stephanie Luciano.{{Cite web|title=Stephanie Rondinha substitui Nicolle Caliari e encara estreante Carol Foro no UFC Seattle|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/stephanie-rondinha-substitui-nicolle-caliari-e-encara-estreante-carol-foro-no-ufc-seattle/|date=2026-01-29|access-date=2026-01-29|publisher=agfight.com|author=Neri Fung}} {{in lang|pt-br}} Foro then withdrew as well and was replaced by Alexia Thainara.{{Cite web|url=https://clutchpoints.com/ufc/ufc-seattle-undergoes-late-strawweight-shuffle-as-alexia-thainara-steps-in-to-face-stephanie-luciano|title=UFC Seattle undergoes late strawweight shuffle as Alexia Thainara steps in to face Stephanie Luciano|publisher=clutchpoints.com|author=Garrett Kerman|date=2025-02-18|access-date=2026-02-20}} According to Foro's team, she tested positive for a diuretic that is not on the approved substance list during an out‑of‑competition drug test earlier in the year, which they stated was not taken intentionally.{{Cite web|url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2026/02/20/ufc-seattle-debut-scrapped-as-carol-foro-announces-positive-drug-test-in-statement/|title=UFC Seattle debut scrapped as Carol Foro announces positive drug test in statement|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Kyle Dimond|date=2026-02-20|access-date=2026-02-20}} Subsequently, Luciano withdrew due to an undisclosed injury and was replaced by Bruna Brasil, setting up a rematch between Brasil and Thainara.{{Cite web|title=Stephanie Rondinha se machuca e deixa luta contra Alexia Burguesinha no UFC Seattle|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/stephanie-rondinha-se-machuca-e-deixa-luta-contra-alexia-burguesinha-no-ufc-seattle/|date=2026-03-02|access-date=2026-03-04|publisher=agfight.com|author=Neri Fung}} {{in lang|pt-br}}{{Cite web|title=Dança das cadeiras! Bruna Brasil substitui Rondinha e encara Alexia Burguesinha no UFC Seattle|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/danca-das-cadeiras-bruna-brasil-substitui-rondinha-e-encara-alexia-burguesinha-no-ufc-seattle/|date=2026-03-05|access-date=2026-03-05|publisher=agfight.com|author=Neri Fung}} {{in lang|pt-br}} The two previously fought at an independent promotion in November 2019, where Brasil won by guillotine‑choke submission in the third round.{{Cite web|title=Quem é Alexia Thainara, brasileira de destaque no card principal do UFC?|url=https://www.lance.com.br/mais-esportes/quem-e-alexia-thainara-brasileira-de-destaque-no-card-principal-do-ufc.html|date=2025-03-22|access-date=2026-03-05|publisher=lance.com.br|author=João Pedro Rodrigues}} {{in lang|pt-br}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Marcin Tybura]] and [[Valter Walker]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/ufc-paramount-plus-news-hub-fight-cards-schedule/413469/fight-news-valter-walker-vs-marcin-tybura-booked-for-ufc-seattle-on-march-28|title=Fight news: Valter Walker vs. Marcin Tybura booked for UFC Seattle on March 28|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Alexander Behunin|date=2026-01-09|access-date=2026-03-05}} However, Walker withdrew due to a foot fracture and was replaced by promotional newcomer Tyrell Fortune.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.pl/rywal-marcina-tybury-kontuzjowany-co-teraz-z-walka-polaka-w-ufc/|title=Rywal Marcina Tybury kontuzjowany. Co teraz z walka Polaka w UFC?|publisher=mma.pl|author=Oskar Wojewodka|date=2026-03-06|access-date=2026-03-06}} {{in lang|pl}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Live]] lightweight winner [[Michael Chiesa]] and [[Carlston Harris]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/ufc/466691/ufc-seattle-veteran-welterweight-books-opponent-for-homecoming-fight|title=UFC Seattle: Veteran welterweight books opponent for homecoming fight|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2026-01-18|access-date=2026-03-14}} However, Harris withdrew due to visa issues and was replaced by [[Niko Price]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/03/13/ufc-seattle-michael-chiesa-vs-carlston-harris-off/89147973007/|title=Michael Chiesa needs new UFC Seattle retirement fight event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/02/09/ufc-tofiq-musayev-vs-samuel-sanches-rizin/88598610007/|title=Former Rizin star Tofiq Musayev booked for second UFC [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on March 14, 2026, at the [[UFC Apex|Meta Apex]] in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas Valley]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2025/12/05/ufc-2026-schedule-first-quarter/87630149007/|title=UFC announces 2026 schedule for first quarter|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2025-12-05|access-date=2025-12-06}}\n\n==Background==\nA featherweight bout between former interim [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] challenger [[Josh Emmett]] and [[Kevin Vallejos]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|title=UFC news: Kevin Vallejos lands first main event vs. Josh Emmett|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/01/16/ufc-news-main-event-kevin-vallejos-vs-josh-emmett/88218580007|date=2026-01-16|access-date=2026-01-16|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}} With Vallejos at age 24 and Emmett at 41, the headliner featured an age difference of 16 years, 9 months and 4 days — the largest ever for a UFC main event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/03/11/ufc-vegas-114-pre-event-facts-record-main-event-age-gap/89097485007/|title=UFC Fight Night 269 pre-event facts: Record-setting main event age gap|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2026-03-11|access-date=2026-03-12}} This was later surpassed in September 2026 at [[UFC Fight Night: Rosas Jr. vs. Barcelos]], where the age gap was 17 years, 5 months and 7 days.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/50039772/barcelos-stretchered-hospitalized-tko-loss-ufc-main-event|title=UFC's Raoni Barcelos 'stable, alert' after TKO loss in main event|publisher=espn.com.au|author=Jeff Wagenheim|date=2026-09-27|access-date=2026-09-28}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] challenger [[Amanda Lemos]] and [[Gillian Robertson]] was originally expected to take place at [[UFC on ESPN: Royval vs. Kape]] in December 2025, but Lemos was removed from the bout by the [[Nevada Athletic Commission]] on the day of the event due to a mouth injury. They met at this event instead.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Amanda-Lemos-vs-Gillian-Robertson-Postponed-to-March-14-199420|title=Amanda Lemos vs. Gillian Robertson postponed to produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on April 18, 2026, at the [[Canada Life Centre]] in [[Winnipeg, Manitoba]], Canada.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/ufc-moving-fight-card-city-winnipeg-canada-waited-10-years-return|title=UFC reportedly moving fight card to city that's waited almost 10 years for UFC return|publisher=si.com|author=Drew Beaupre|date=2026-02-20|access-date=2026-02-20}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's third visit to Winnipeg and first since [[UFC on Fox: Lawler vs. dos Anjos]] in December 2017. There were initial reports that the event was expected to take place in [[Ottawa, Ontario]], but it was later shifted to Winnipeg for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.si.com/fannation/mma/news/pivotal-fight-jasudavicius-silva-first-ufc-ottawa-card-7-years|title=Pivotal UFC contender fight booked for first UFC Ottawa card in nearly 7 years|publisher=si.com|author=Drew Beaupre|date=2026-02-13|access-date=2026-02-13}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger [[Gilbert Burns]] and [[Mike Malott]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|author=Mike Bohn|title=Gilbert Burns vs. Mike Malott headlines UFC Fight Night 273 in Winnipeg|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/02/20/ufc-returns-winnipeg-gilbert-burns-vs-mike-malott-main-event/88782064007/|access-date=2026-02-20|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}}\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Road to UFC Season 3#Flyweight|Road to UFC Season 3]] flyweight winner Choi Dong-hun and undefeated prospect André Lima was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmacrossfire.com/2026/02/16/ufc-ottawa-canadian-womens-flyweight-jasmine-jasudavicius-returns-against-karine-silva/|title=UFC Ottawa: Canadian women's flyweight Jasmine Jasudavicius returns against Karine Silva|publisher=mmacrossfire.com|author=Kenai Andrews|date=2026-02-16|access-date=2026-02-18}} The bout was originally scheduled to take place at [[UFC 327]] one week before, but was moved to this event for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=http://www.pwinsiderxtra.com/ViewArticle.php?id=39764&p=1|title=Report: Andre Lima Faces Dong Hun Choi at UFC 327|publisher=pwinsiderxtra.com|author=David Tees|date=2026-01-24|access-date=2026-01-25}} In turn, Choi withdrew due to an injury, so the bout was scrapped and Lima was booked for a bout at a later date.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mk.co.kr/en/sports/11996567|title=Choi Dong-hoon (27, Pohang Team Mad) will be postponed for the first time on the global MMA No. 1 stage|publisher=mk.co.kr|author=Kang Lee|date=2026-05-08|access-date=2026-05-09}}\n\nDuring the event's broadcast, former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Chris Weidman]] was announced as the next \"modern wing\" [[UFC Hall of Fame]] inductee during International Fight Week festivities in Las Vegas in July. Also announced, longtime UFC writer Thomas Gerbasi will be inducted into the \"contributor wing\". His entrance comes posthumously after he died on September 16, 2025, at 57. Gerbasi was a longtime combat sports writer who served as the UFC.com editorial director for over 20 years and also worked for [[The Ring (magazine)|The Ring]] and [[BoxingScene]].{{Cite
